Requirement: remove commas but keep leading currency symbol.\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        value = str(value)\n    if value.startswith(\"₩\"):\n        return \"₩\" + value[1:].replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return value.replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n\ndef months_inclusive(sy: int, sm: int, ey: int, em: int) -> int:\n    return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm + 1)\n\n\ndef parse_month_span(text: str) -> Optional[int]:\n    \"\"\"Extract a (N개월) month span from a date range inside the phase text.\n\n    Supports patterns commonly seen in our JSON/template:\n    - (YYYY.MM ~ YYYY.MM)\n    - (YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM)\n    - (YYYY.MM) / (YYYY-MM)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Prefer explicit ranges first\n    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm, ey, em = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, ey, em)\n\n    # Single month in parentheses: (YYYY.MM) or (YYYY-MM)\n    m = re.search(r\"\\((\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\)\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, sy, sm)\n\n    # Also allow without parentheses (defensive)\n    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, sy, sm)\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef should_remove_cache_element(tag: str) -> bool:\n    ln = localname(tag).lower()\n    # HWPX stores line segmentation / charshape caches that become stale.\n    return (\n        \"lineseg\" in ln\n        or ln == \"linesegarray\"\n        or ln == \"linesegarray\"  # variant casing handled by lower()\n        or \"charshapearray\" in ln\n    )\n\n\ndef iter_modified_text_nodes(root: ET.Element) -> Iterable[ET.Element]:\n    # HWPX text nodes are typically hp:t within hp:run within hp:p.\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) == \"t\":\n            yield el\n\n\ndef find_parent_map(root: ET.Element) -> Dict[ET.Element, ET.Element]:\n    return {child: parent for parent in root.iter() for child in parent}\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_el: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove layout-cache descendant elements within a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    parent_map = find_parent_map(p_el)\n    to_remove: List[ET.Element] = []\n    for el in list(p_el.iter()):\n        if el is p_el:\n            continue\n        if should_remove_cache_element(el.tag):\n            to_remove.append(el)\n\n    removed = 0\n    for el in to_remove:\n        parent = parent_map.get(el)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(el)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Replace all {{key}} placeholders in a string.\n\n    Returns (new_text, changed).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in data:\n            raise KeyError(f\"No JSON value for placeholder: {key}\")\n        val = data[key]\n        if key == \"예산\":\n            val = normalize_budget(val)\n        changed = True\n        return str(val)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\ndef append_month_span_if_phase_line(text: str) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"If this is a 단계 line, append (N개월) computed from its date range.\"\"\"\n\n    # Only apply to the phase lines, per requirements.\n    if not re.search(r\"\\b[123]단계\\b\", text):\n        return text, False\n\n    # Avoid double-appending.\n    if re.search(r\"\\(\\d+개월\\)\\s*$\", text):\n        return text, False\n\n    months = parse_month_span(text)\n    if months is None:\n        return text, False\n\n    return f\"{text} ({months}개월)\", True\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(path: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file. Returns True if modified.\"\"\"\n\n    tree = ET.parse(path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paragraphs: set[ET.Element] = set()\n\n    parent_map = find_parent_map(root)\n\n    # 1) Replace placeholders in hp:t nodes\n    for t_el in iter_modified_text_nodes(root):\n        if t_el.text and \"{{\" in t_el.text:\n            new_text, changed = replace_placeholders_in_text(t_el.text, data)\n            if changed and new_text != t_el.text:\n                t_el.text = new_text\n                # find enclosing paragraph (<hp:p>)\n                p = t_el\n                while p is not None and localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n                    p = parent_map.get(p)\n                if p is not None:\n                    modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n\n    # 2) Append month spans to phase lines (after replacements)\n    for t_el in iter_modified_text_nodes(root):\n        if t_el.text and re.search(r\"\\b[123]단계\\b\", t_el.text):\n            new_text, changed = append_month_span_if_phase_line(t_el.text)\n            if changed and new_text != t_el.text:\n                t_el.text = new_text\n                p = t_el\n                while p is not None and localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n                    p = parent_map.get(p)\n                if p is not None:\n                    modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n\n    # 3) Remove stale layout-cache elements from modified paragraphs\n    for p in modified_paragraphs:\n        remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p)\n\n    # Ensure no placeholders remain in this file\n    xml_bytes = ET.tostring(root, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n    if b\"{{\" in xml_bytes:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Placeholders remain after processing: {path}\")\n\n    # Write back (preserve declaration/encoding)\n    with open(path, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(xml_bytes)\n\n    return bool(modified_paragraphs)\n\n\ndef zip_dir(src_dir: str, out_path: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create a .hwpx ZIP from a directory (paths relative to src_dir).\"\"\"\n\n    # Prefer explicit ranges first\n    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm, ey, em = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, ey, em)\n\n    # Requirement: remove commas but keep leading currency symbol.\n38\t    if not isinstance(value, str):\n39\t        value = str(value)\n40\t    if value.startswith(\"₩\"):\n41\t        return \"₩\" + value[1:].replace(\",\", \"\")\n42\t    return value.replace(\",\", \"\")\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef months_inclusive(sy: int, sm: int, ey: int, em: int) -> int:\n46\t    return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm + 1)\n47\t\n48\t\n49\tdef parse_month_span(text: str) -> Optional[int]:\n50\t    \"\"\"Extract a (N개월) month span from a date range inside the phase text.\n51\t\n52\t    Supports patterns commonly seen in our JSON/template:\n53\t    - (YYYY.MM ~ YYYY.MM)\n54\t    - (YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM)\n55\t    - (YYYY.MM) / (YYYY-MM)\n56\t    \"\"\"\n57\t\n58\t    # Prefer explicit ranges first\n59\t    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n60\t    if m:\n61\t        sy, sm, ey, em = map(int, m.groups())\n62\t        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, ey, em)\n63\t\n64\t    # HWPX line/charshape cache entries become stale after any text edit.\n    # The task requires clearing them for modified paragraphs; in practice it's\n    # safer to clear them for all paragraphs in a file that was edited so the\n    # Requirement: remove commas but keep leading currency symbol.\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        value = str(value)\n    if value.startswith(\"₩\"):\n        return \"₩\" + value[1:].replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return value.replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n\ndef months_inclusive(sy: int, sm: int, ey: int, em: int) -> int:\n    return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm + 1)\n\n\ndef parse_month_span(text: str) -> Optional[int]:\n    \"\"\"Extract a (N개월) month span from a date range inside the phase text.\n\n    Supports patterns commonly seen in our JSON/template:\n    - (YYYY.MM ~ YYYY.MM)\n    - (YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM)\n    - (YYYY.MM) / (YYYY-MM)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Prefer explicit ranges first\n    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm, ey, em = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, ey, em)\n\n    # Single month in parentheses: (YYYY.MM) or (YYYY-MM)\n    m = re.search(r\"\\((\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\\)\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, sy, sm)\n\n    # Also allow without parentheses (defensive)\n    m = re.search(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text)\n    if m:\n        sy, sm = map(int, m.groups())\n        return months_inclusive(sy, sm, sy, sm)\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef should_remove_cache_element(tag: str) -> bool:\n    ln = localname(tag).lower()\n    # HWPX stores line segmentation / charshape caches that become stale.\n    return (\n        \"lineseg\" in ln\n        or ln == \"linesegarray\"\n        or ln == \"linesegarray\"  # variant casing handled by lower()\n        or \"charshapearray\" in ln\n    )\n\n\ndef iter_modified_text_nodes(root: ET.Element) -> Iterable[ET.Element]:\n    # HWPX text nodes are typically hp:t within hp:run within hp:p.\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) == \"t\":\n            yield el\n\n\ndef find_parent_map(root: ET.Element) -> Dict[ET.Element, ET.Element]:\n    return {child: parent for parent in root.iter() for child in parent}\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_el: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove layout-cache descendant elements within a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    parent_map = find_parent_map(p_el)\n    to_remove: List[ET.Element] = []\n    for el in list(p_el.iter()):\n        if el is p_el:\n            continue\n        if should_remove_cache_element(el.tag):\n            to_remove.append(el)\n\n    removed = 0\n    for el in to_remove:\n        parent = parent_map.get(el)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(el)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Replace all {{key}} placeholders in a string.\n\n    Returns (new_text, changed).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key not in data:\n            raise KeyError(f\"No JSON value for placeholder: {key}\")\n        val = data[key]\n        if key == \"예산\":\n            val = normalize_budget(val)\n        changed = True\n        return str(val)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\ndef append_month_span_if_phase_line(text: str) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"If this is a 단계 line, append (N개월) computed from its date range.\"\"\"\n\n    # Only apply to the phase lines, per requirements.\n    if not re.search(r\"\\b[123]단계\\b\", text):\n        return text, False\n\n    # Avoid double-appending.\n    if re.search(r\"\\(\\d+개월\\)\\s*$\", text):\n        return text, False\n\n    months = parse_month_span(text)\n    if months is None:\n        return text, False\n\n    return f\"{text} ({months}개월)\", True\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(path: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file. Returns True if modified.\"\"\"\n\n    tree = ET.parse(path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paragraphs: set[ET.Element] = set()\n\n    parent_map = find_parent_map(root)\n\n    # 1) Replace placeholders in hp:t nodes\n    for t_el in iter_modified_text_nodes(root):\n        if t_el.text and \"{{\" in t_el.text:\n            new_text, changed = replace_placeholders_in_text(t_el.text, data)\n            if changed and new_text != t_el.text:\n                t_el.text = new_text\n                #
